With zero costs the plain V is about break-even, so the spread + $0.06 commission is what sinks it. Expected live: auto-pause after ~30 closes if still negative. 0.01 lot · tag PING1 · PING1 v1.1 (Owner's V on tiny timeframes): direction = snap-back after a stretched M4 bar (RSI(2) of M4 < 5 -> up, > 95 -> down, valid 3 M4 bars); entry at the M3 'V' turn - the previous M3 bar made the 5-bar low, this M3 bar is green, closes above the previous bar's high with a higher low -> BUY (inverted V -> SELL). 9 lowest-spread pairs, entries ALL HOURS FX is open (no entries 4:45-6:15 PM ET rollover or Friday after 3:30 PM); 0.01 lot, hard SL 20x ATR14(M3) (max $3), no fixed TP: Owner's net trailing ladder moves the broker SL up (+$0.03 -> lock $0.01 ... +$1 -> $0.80, then 80% of peak) with breathing room 3x ATR14(M1); time stop 8 h; max 6 open, 1 per pair; tag PING1 · runner running · placing ON · last check Fri 10/9 6:48 AM ET
Entry: a ‘V’ on tiny timeframes. First an M4 (4-minute) bar is stretched: RSI(2) below 5 means look for a bounce up, above 95 means look for a drop (valid for 3 M4 bars). Then on M3 (3-minute) bars: the previous bar made the lowest low of the last 5 bars, and the new bar is green, closes above the previous bar’s high with a higher low → BUY (upside-down V → SELL). Pairs: 9 lowest-spread pairs (EURUSD, GBPUSD, USDJPY, USDCHF, USDCAD, AUDUSD, NZDUSD, EURGBP, EURCHF). Hours: all hours FX is open, except no entries 4:45–6:15 PM ET (rollover) and Friday after 3:30 PM. Size: 0.01 lot, max 6 open, 1 per pair. Ladder: once a trade is up +$0.03 net it locks +$0.01, rising to lock $0.80 at +$1, then 80% of the peak; the broker SL is moved up (or a virtual lock closes at market). Stop: hard broker stop 20× ATR(M3), at most $3 loss; time stop 8 h. Auto-stop: pauses at −$150 closed, 6 losses in a row, or still negative after 30 closes (now: $156.08 room, 5 losses-in-a-row left).

Backtest (honest; simulated, not live trades): HistData M1 BID bars Apr-Sep 2026 (6 months, 13 crosses; only bid M1 data exists on the box - no historical ask or ticks; live median spreads from the 10/8 reality check used as cost). Filled in the SAME minute as the gap: 655 trades, net +50.22 USD, 52.7% green, PF 1.36 (A 40 trades +13.01; Z 615 trades +37.21). Filled ONE minute later: 634 trades, net -65.05 USD, 45.3% green, PF 0.61. So the gaps exist (about 5 a day across the 13 crosses) but the edge is gone if the fill is ~1 minute late; live fills take ~5-10 s through the terminal, so expect somewhere in between and likely around break-even or worse. M1 closes are not simultaneous across pairs, which makes the same-minute result optimistic. Armed anyway on Owner order 1:19 PM ET 10/8.
Reality check (read-only scan, no orders) (1:23–1:43 PM ET 10/8, 20 min, 379 reads of 13 crosses): 379 live reads over 20 min (median read 802 ms). A cross's gap beat its own cost (spread + commission) on 23.0% of cross-reads, but almost all of that is a STANDING broker offset, not a passing mistake: EURCAD -3.1 p on 100% of reads, CADJPY +2.2 p on 100% of reads, AUDCAD -1.9 p on 100% of reads (USDCAD.sim is priced ~2.5 p away from what EURCAD, AUDCAD and CADJPY imply, all the time). Fresh gaps beyond cost + 0.5 p: 0.0% of cross-reads. Under the armed rules the window would have fired trigger A 0 time(s) and trigger Z 0 time(s) (counted per read, so one episode can count several times). Every other cross: median gap 0.11 p vs cost 1.66 p. |z| reached 3+ on 12 of 13 crosses (biggest: AUDJPY z -4.3, EURAUD -4.0, EURCHF -3.8), but those moves were only 0.3-1.0 pip while cost was 1.1-2.8 pips, so none was big enough to pay for the trade. Expect very few trades.

0.01 lot · tag MISTAKE1 · Pricing-mistake hunter (FX version of two-pool arbitrage; no direction guess). Every ~3 s it reads the broker's own bid/ask for the 7 USD majors and 13 crosses from the MT5 terminal, works out what each cross SHOULD cost from its two USD legs (e.g. EURJPY = EURUSD x USDJPY), and measures the gap in pips. Trigger A (absolute mistake): the gap is bigger than the cross's spread + the $0.06 commission (in pips) + 0.5 pip safety, on 2 reads in a row, and it is NEW (a gap that has sat there for the last ~10 min is a standing broker offset, not a mistake - e.g. USDCAD.sim runs ~2.5 pips above what the CAD crosses imply). Trigger Z (statistical): the gap is 3+ standard deviations from its own ~10-minute average AND that move beats spread + commission. It then trades ONLY the cross, 0.01 lot (broker minimum), in the direction that closes the gap. Exit: as soon as the gap closes (back to zero for A, back to its average for Z) and the trade is at least +$0.01 after commission; or Jim's ladder (peak +$0.03 locks +$0.01, +$0.09 -> $0.045, +$0.15 -> $0.10, +$0.30 -> $0.15, +$0.50 -> $0.25, +$1.00 -> $0.80, above $1 keeps 80%; checked every ~3 s, closes at market); or 15 minutes; or the broker hard stop at 2x the entry gap (at least 2x spread + 1 pip, max $1). Max 3 open, 1 per cross. Auto-stop at -$150 or 5 losses in a row. All hours FX is open; Friday 4:50 PM ET flatten (no entries after 4:35). FTMO guards: equity below day-start - $8,000 or below $182,000 -> flatten, no entries. · runner running · placing ON · last check Fri 10/9 6:48 AM ET
No trades yet, so every $0.00 / 0% below is a real zero, not missing data. Co-op 1 — behaviour library

passed updated not yet
Every pair + timeframe + setup is a behaviour. After 8 or more closes: net < $0 or < 60% green → FAILED (stored, never traded again); net > $0 and ≥ 75% green → PASSED (preferred when several fire). Files: research/coop1/LIBRARY_PASSED.json and LIBRARY_FAILED.json.

no closes yet — 18 behaviours (9 pairs × M3/M5) waiting for their first trade
Backtest (honest; simulated, not live trades): CO-OP 1 backtest (HistData M1 Apr-2025..Sep-2026, 9 low-spread pairs, real cost model: per-pair spread x session, $0.06 commission per 0.01, 0.2 p entry slip, 0.3 p stop slip, swap; Jim's ladder, no-progress 30 min, max hold 240 min, $2 stop cap, max 4 open, currency cap 2, recovery mode). HONEST: CLEARLY NEGATIVE. Without the library: OUT-OF-SAMPLE Jan-Sep 2026 3564 trades, 40.2% green, net $-530.35, PF 0.233, median $-0.033, max DD $530.44, longest losing streak 15; in-sample 3771 trades, 43.6% green, net $-589.47, PF 0.275, median $-0.020, max DD $589.6, longest losing streak 12. With the behaviour library it benches all 18 pair+timeframe behaviours after 182 trades (57.7% green, net $-25.47, max DD $25.61) and then stops trading - the library protects the account but finds no keeper. With zero costs the same entries are 79.4% green, PF 1.059 (net $+53.46) - the spread + commission is what sinks it, same as PING1. Consistency checklist would FAIL (PF, green %, losing streak). Launched live anyway at 0.01 on Jim's order.
0.01 lot · tag COOP1 · Co-op 1 (consistency under pressure, 0.01 lot FIXED, no scaling). 9 low-spread pairs, two setups (M3 with M4 dip, M5 with M15 dip). Entry = V-turn equilibrium: DIP (RSI(2) of the direction TF < 5 or > 95 in its last 3 bars and a 5-bar low/high), RISE (next bar closes through the dip bar by >= 0.2 x ATR, in its outer third, higher low/lower high), SETTLE (next bar holds above the turn bar's low/below its high, closes on the right side of its midpoint, range <= turn bar range), then ENTER at market. Volatility filter: skip when ATR(14) is in the top 20% of its last 100 bars. Boat (balance governor): max 4 open, 1 per pair, no more than 2 open trades leaning the same way on any one currency; while the test's total-if-closed is below $0 no new entries except one recovery trade when flat. Exits: hard stop 3 x ATR14 of the entry TF, never more than $2 incl. commission; Jim's trailing ladder with breathing room (+$0.03 -> lock $0.01, $0.15 -> $0.10, $0.30 -> $0.15, $0.50 -> $0.25, $1.00 -> $0.80, then 80% of peak); no-progress exit if +$0.03 not reached in 30 min; max hold 4 h; Friday 4:00 PM ET flatten. Auto-stop: -$150 closed or 5 losses in a row -> flatten and pause (awaiting orders). FTMO guards: equity < Prague day-start - $8,000 or < $182,000 -> flatten, no entries. Hours: all FX hours except 4:45-6:15 PM ET rollover; Friday no entries from 3:30 PM ET. Behaviour library: each pair|TF|setup is a behaviour; after >= 8 closes, net < $0 or < 60% green -> FAILED (benched, never traded again); net > $0 and >= 75% green -> PASSED (preferred). AI Consistent — behaviour library

updated Fri 6:48:42 AM ET
Every behaviour carries a running estimate of net $ per trade (history prior worth 8 trades + live results, per 0.01 lot) and green rate. When its setup fires, a random draw from those estimates decides: trade if $/trade > 0 and green ≥ 60%; 5% exploration while untested. PASSED: 10+ closes, net > $0, ≥ 75% green, median > $0. DEMOTED: 8+ closes, average < $0 (2% exploration). FAILED: 12+ closes, net < $0 and < 60% green (retired). Files: research/aicon/LIBRARY_PASSED.json, LIBRARY_FAILED.json, POOL.json.

5 · Details

Three methods in one book, each order tagged with its own comment so results split per method. V (BASKETV2V) = the Ping 1 V-turn on M3. R (BASKETV2R) = RSI(2) on M15 below 5 while H1 is above its 50-EMA (a sharp dip against an up-trend: buy it), or above 95 while H1 is below its 50-EMA (a spike against a down-trend: sell it). X (BASKETV2X) = Mistake-style: a cross's price drifts away from the price implied by its two USD legs by more than costs; trade it back, exit on convergence (net >= +$0.01) or after 15 min, stop 2x the gap (max $1). At most one trade per method per pair, max 8 open, 0.01 lot.

Fix notes

fixed Fri 5:49:17 AM ET
Why paused: Fixed 10/9 (v2.1: V and X dropped, R flipped to stretch-fade, 2-4 PM ET only) - paused, awaiting Jim's orders
Verdict: v2.1 is small but positive in-sample and out-of-sample, including with spreads doubled. Lean yes, re-arm at 0.01 on Jim's order.

What was wrong

  • Method V is the same entry as Ping 1, which is already live, and it is negative in the backtest (OOS PF 0.68). On 10/8 it made 2 trades: +$0.12 and -$0.50.
  • Method R bought dips against the H1 trend at all hours and is negative in the backtest (OOS PF 0.71). Its one trade on 10/8 was EURCHF at 4:00 PM ET, carried through rollover (-$0.10 swap), then closed by the run window: -$1.25.
  • Method X could never fire. The gap between a cross and its implied price is 1-2 pips, but costs on this broker are 2-30 pips. It had 0 triggers in 2,031 reads, and Mistake 1 already scans this.
  • Both losing trades were opened 4:00-4:36 PM ET, inside the rollover hour, and paid swap.

What was changed

  • V and X dropped.
  • R is flipped to the AGGR1-style fade: buy an M15 RSI(2) below 5 only while H1 is below its 50-EMA; sell the mirror case.
  • New entries only 2:00-4:00 PM ET. No entries 4:45-6:15 PM ET or on Friday after 3:30 PM ET.
  • Stop is 5x ATR(M15), max $3 at 0.01. Time exit after 4 h. Ladder breathing room is 2x ATR(M1). 0.01 lot, max 6 open.
